## Stage 1: Scope And Red Tests
**Goal**: Lock the first conservative Phase 3.3 slice to small and medium endpoint modules with proven DB exception contracts and add failing tests that pin desired error-mapping behavior before refactoring.
**Success Criteria**: Tranche-one scope is limited to mapper-ready endpoints; `workspaces.py` and `chat_grammars.py` are in scope, while `tools.py`, `personalization.py`, and giant files are deferred when their exception behavior does not fit the shared DB mapper cleanly.
**Tests**: `python -m pytest tldw_Server_API/tests/Utils/test_api_v1_utils.py -k http_error_mapping -v`; targeted endpoint tests for workspace and chat grammar routes.
**Status**: Complete

## Stage 2: Adopt Shared Error Mapping In Small Endpoints
**Goal**: Refactor small endpoint modules that already expose ChaCha/media DB exception contracts to use the shared mapper where tests justify it.
**Success Criteria**: `chat_grammars.py` stops leaking raw `CharactersRAGDBError` text for covered create/list/get/update/delete paths while preserving the delete-path `404` `InputError` behavior and the delete conflict `409` detail; `chat_documents.py` now routes the covered document-generate `InputError` path through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `400` detail string; `items.py` maps covered legacy media `InputError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper; `media/listing.py` now routes the covered media-keywords `InputError` path through the shared DB HTTP mapper so invalid keyword filters surface as `400` while preserving the existing `500` detail for base database failures; `media/listing.py` now also routes the covered media-search `InputError` and `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `400` detail strings, the existing search-specific `500` detail string, and the surrounding `422`/generic-error behavior; `sync.py` now routes the covered `/sync/get` `DatabaseError` path through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing sync-specific `500` detail string and leaving the raw `sqlite3.Error` and generic sanitization paths untouched; `notes_graph.py` routes covered graph/link `InputError` and `CharactersRAGDBError` paths through the shared mapper while preserving the custom duplicate-link conflict response; the remediation-conversion subsection of `quizzes.py` routes covered `InputError`/`ConflictError`/`CharactersRAGDBError` paths through the shared mapper without widening into unrelated quiz handlers; the quiz study-assistant context and respond paths now map covered ChaCha `InputError` cases to `400` instead of leaking or collapsing them into the generic `500` fallback; the flashcard study-assistant context and respond paths now map covered ChaCha `InputError` cases to `400` instead of leaking or collapsing them into the generic `500` fallback while preserving the custom stale-thread `409`; `feedback.py` now maps covered feedback-store `CharactersRAGDBError` failures in list/delete/update routes through the shared DB HTTP mapper instead of leaking raw exceptions; `media/reprocess.py` now maps covered media DB `InputError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper in both chunking and embeddings-only flows while preserving existing `ConflictError`/`DatabaseError` detail text; `character_memory.py` now maps covered list/archive/create/update/delete paths plus the shared fetch helper through the shared DB HTTP mapper for pinned `InputError` and `CharactersRAGDBError` cases instead of leaking raw exceptions; `media/item.py` now maps the covered detail-read `DatabaseError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail string; `media/item.py` now maps the covered update-path `InputError` case to `400` through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail for database failures; `media/item.py` now also maps the covered trash/delete-path `InputError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail for database failures; `media/item.py` now also maps the covered restore-path `InputError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail for database failures; `media/item.py` now also maps the covered permanent-delete `InputError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail for database failures; `media/item.py` now routes the covered keyword-update `DatabaseError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail string and the `404` `InputError` behavior; `media/versions.py` now maps the covered list/get read-path `DatabaseError` cases through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail string; `media/versions.py` now maps the covered rollback-path `InputError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper before the generic `ValueError` fallback while preserving the existing `500` detail for database failures; `media/versions.py` now also maps the covered delete-path `InputError` case through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail and custom `409` conflict response; `media/versions.py` now prechecks media existence in the covered create-path so missing media still returns the established `404`, while the remaining DB `InputError` path is mapped through the shared DB HTTP mapper without changing the existing `500` database fallback; `email.py` now routes the covered search/detail `InputError` and `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `400` details, `500` details, and database-error logging; `web_clipper.py` now routes the covered save/status/enrichment DB-exception paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the save-path `409`, the status/enrichment `404` conflict remap, and the generic `500` detail for base ChaCha failures; `prompt_studio_projects.py` now routes the covered update/archive/unarchive `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `404` `InputError` behavior and endpoint-specific `500` detail strings; `prompt_studio_projects.py` now also routes the covered create/list/get `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing create/get `500` detail strings and the list-projects test-mode detail suffix; `prompt_studio_projects.py` now also routes the covered stats-path `DatabaseError` through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail string; `prompt_studio_test_cases.py` now routes the covered update/delete `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing update-path `404` `InputError` behavior and endpoint-specific `500` detail strings; `prompt_studio_test_cases.py` now also routes the covered create/list/get `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing create/get `500` detail strings and the list-test-cases test-mode detail suffix; `prompt_studio_test_cases.py` now also routes the covered bulk-create `ConflictError`/`DatabaseError` path through the shared DB HTTP mapper so bulk conflicts surface as `409` while preserving the existing generic `500` detail for base database failures; `prompt_studio_prompts.py` now routes the covered create/list/get `DatabaseError` paths through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing prompt-specific `500` detail strings and leaving the surrounding `ConflictError` and `InputError` handling unchanged; `prompt_studio_prompts.py` now also routes the covered update-path `DatabaseError` through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail string and leaving the surrounding input-validation behavior unchanged; `prompt_studio_prompts.py` now also routes the covered history-path `DatabaseError` through the shared DB HTTP mapper while preserving the existing `500` detail string and leaving the surrounding `InputError` handling unchanged; `prompt_studio_prompts.py` now also routes the covered revert-path `ConflictError`/`DatabaseError` through the shared DB HTTP mapper so prompt-version conflicts surface as `409` while preserving the existing `500` detail for base database failures; targeted tests pass with no behavior regression outside the pinned cases; `tools.py`, `personalization.py`, and the remaining broader `quizzes.py`/`flashcards.py` handlers stay deferred until their error contracts are narrowed.

## Stage 3: Adopt Shared Error Mapping In Medium Endpoint
**Goal**: Refactor `workspaces.py` to use the shared mapper for covered database and service exception paths without expanding into giant-file cleanup.
**Success Criteria**: Covered workspace exception paths use consistent mapped HTTP responses; the conservative slice includes the tested `patch_workspace`, `delete_workspace`, `update_source`, `update_artifact`, and `update_note` paths; `agent_orchestration.py`, `media/item.py`, and `workflows.py` remain untouched in tranche one.
**Tests**: Targeted workspace endpoint tests and any new regression tests for 404/409/500 mappings.
**Status**: Complete
